---
[
  {
    "id": "tokyo-shibuya",
    "name": {
      "zh": "东京涩谷",
      "en": "Tokyo Shibuya"
    },
    "description": {
      "zh": "涩谷十字路口是全球最繁忙的十字路口之一，每次绿灯约有3,000人同时穿行。周边聚集了东京最前沿的时尚、美食和娱乐，是现代都市文化的缩影。",
      "en": "Shibuya Crossing is one of the world's busiest intersections — some 3,000 people cross at once on each green light. The surrounding district is Tokyo's epicentre of fashion, food and urban pop culture."
    },
    "image": "https://picsum.photos/seed/tokyo-shibuya/800/500",
    "tags": {
      "zh": [
        "都市",
        "购物",
        "夜生活"
      ],
      "en": [
        "Urban",
        "Shopping",
        "Nightlife"
      ]
    },
    "location": {
      "zh": "东京都涩谷区",
      "en": "Shibuya, Tokyo"
    },
    "highlights": {
      "zh": [
        "日均逾100万人次的涩谷十字路口",
        "忠犬八公像，东京最著名的集合地点",
        "涩谷Sky展望台360°俯瞰东京夜景"
      ],
      "en": [
        "Shibuya Crossing sees over 1 million people daily",
        "Hachiko statue — Tokyo's most famous meeting spot",
        "Shibuya Sky observation deck with 360° night views"
      ]
    },
    "admission": {
      "zh": "街区免费，涩谷Sky展望台约2,000日元",
      "en": "Street free; Shibuya Sky observation deck ~¥2,000"
    },
    "duration": {
      "zh": "半天至一天",
      "en": "Half to full day"
    },
    "tips": {
      "zh": "涩谷十字路口最佳观景点是涩谷站二楼星巴克或对面的 Mag's Park 观景台，无需消费。",
      "en": "The best free vantage point for the crossing is the second-floor Starbucks in Shibuya Station or the Mag's Park terrace opposite."
    },
    "subdivision": "tokyo"
  },
  {
    "id": "ogasawara",
    "name": {
      "zh": "小笠原群岛",
      "en": "Ogasawara Islands"
    },
    "description": {
      "zh": "小笠原群岛位于东京以南约1,000公里的太平洋上，从未与大陆相连，孕育了大量独一无二的动植物，被称为「东方加拉帕戈斯」。2011年列入UNESCO世界自然遗产。",
      "en": "The Ogasawara Islands, 1,000 km south of Tokyo in the Pacific, have never been connected to a continent and harbour extraordinary endemic species — earning the nickname 'Galapagos of the Orient'. UNESCO Natural Heritage since 2011."
    },
    "image": "https://picsum.photos/seed/ogasawara-japan/800/500",
    "tags": {
      "zh": [
        "世界遗产",
        "自然遗产",
        "生物多样性"
      ],
      "en": [
        "UNESCO Heritage",
        "Natural Heritage",
        "Biodiversity"
      ]
    },
    "location": {
      "zh": "东京都小笠原村（父岛、母岛）",
      "en": "Ogasawara Village, Tokyo (Chichi-jima and Haha-jima)"
    },
    "highlights": {
      "zh": [
        "195种特有植物，大量特有鸟类和昆虫",
        "父岛周边海域：海豚、鲸鱼、绿海龟可近距离观察",
        "与东京都本土仅靠一艘25.5小时航程的定期船连接"
      ],
      "en": [
        "195 endemic plant species, plus endemic birds and insects found nowhere else",
        "Waters around Chichi-jima teem with dolphins, whales and green sea turtles",
        "Accessible only by a single ferry with a 25.5-hour sailing from Tokyo"
      ]
    },
    "admission": {
      "zh": "岛上景点大多免费，须购买往返船票约50,000日元",
      "en": "Most island sites free; round-trip ferry ticket ~¥50,000"
    },
    "duration": {
      "zh": "至少5—7天（含船上时间）",
      "en": "Minimum 5–7 days including sailing time"
    },
    "tips": {
      "zh": "小笠原丸定期船每6天一班，需提前预订；岛上没有空港，时间充裕才能成行。6—8月海况最宜人。",
      "en": "The Ogasawara Maru ferry sails every six days — book well ahead. There is no airport; allow a full week minimum. June–August offers the calmest seas."
    },
    "subdivision": "tokyo"
  },
  {
    "id": "national-museum-western-art",
    "name": {
      "zh": "国立西洋美术馆（勒·柯布西耶建筑作品）",
      "en": "National Museum of Western Art, Tokyo (Le Corbusier)"
    },
    "description": {
      "zh": "东京上野的国立西洋美术馆由建筑大师勒·柯布西耶设计，1959年落成，是其「无限成长博物馆」概念的杰出体现。作为全球17处勒·柯布西耶建筑作品的一部分，于2016年共同列入UNESCO世界遗产。",
      "en": "The National Museum of Western Art in Ueno, Tokyo, designed by Le Corbusier and completed in 1959, is an outstanding realisation of his 'Museum of Unlimited Growth' concept. As one of 17 Le Corbusier works worldwide, it was jointly UNESCO-listed in 2016."
    },
    "image": "https://picsum.photos/seed/nmwa-tokyo-japan/800/500",
    "tags": {
      "zh": [
        "世界遗产",
        "勒·柯布西耶",
        "现代建筑"
      ],
      "en": [
        "UNESCO Heritage",
        "Le Corbusier",
        "Modern Architecture"
      ]
    },
    "location": {
      "zh": "东京都台东区上野公园",
      "en": "Ueno Park, Taito, Tokyo"
    },
    "highlights": {
      "zh": [
        "勒·柯布西耶在亚洲唯一的作品，螺旋形「无限成长」概念",
        "前庭罗丹雕塑园：含《地狱之门》《思想者》等名作",
        "馆藏西方绘画自文艺复兴至20世纪，逾4,500件"
      ],
      "en": [
        "Le Corbusier's only work in Asia; the spiral 'Museum of Unlimited Growth' concept",
        "Foreyard sculpture garden featuring Rodin's Gates of Hell and The Thinker",
        "Collection of over 4,500 Western artworks from the Renaissance to the 20th century"
      ]
    },
    "admission": {
      "zh": "常设展500日元；特别展另行收费",
      "en": "Permanent collection ¥500; special exhibitions extra"
    },
    "duration": {
      "zh": "1.5—2小时",
      "en": "1.5–2 hours"
    },
    "tips": {
      "zh": "上野公园内集中了多处博物馆和美术馆，可安排「上野博物馆巡礼」半日游。每月第二、四个周六免费开放常设展。",
      "en": "Ueno Park clusters several museums — plan a 'Ueno museum crawl' for a rewarding half-day. The permanent collection is free on the 2nd and 4th Saturday of each month."
    },
    "subdivision": "tokyo"
  },
  {
    "id": "starbucks-coffee-shibuya-tsutaya",

      "en": "Starbucks Coffee - Shibuya Tsutaya is a popular café in Tokyo, celebrated for its prime location or distinctive interior — one of Tokyo's most sought-after café experiences for visitors."

      "en": "Can be very busy at peak hours — visit at opening time or on a weekday morning."

      "en": "Toyosu Market is a well-known shopping and commercial landmark in Tokyo, offering a diverse mix of specialty stores and dining — a popular destination for experiencing Japan's urban retail culture."

      "en": "Sensō-ji is a celebrated Buddhist temple in Tokyo with a distinguished history and characteristic architecture, offering visitors an immersive experience of Japan's temple culture."

    "id": "shinjuku-gyoen-national-garden",
    "name": {
      "zh": "新宿御苑",
      "en": "Shinjuku Gyoen National Garden"
    },
    "description": {
      "zh": "新宿御苑是东京都的一处优美园林，巧妙融合了日本传统造园艺术，四季景色各异，是都市中难得的清幽休憩之地。",
      "en": "Shinjuku Gyoen National Garden is a fine garden in Tokyo that elegantly incorporates traditional Japanese landscape design. The grounds transform with each season, offering a peaceful urban retreat."
    },
    "image": "https://picsum.photos/seed/shinjuku-gyoen-national-garden-japan/800/500",
    "tags": {

        "自然"
      ],

        "Japanese Landscaping",

      ]
    },
    "location": {
      "zh": "东京都",
      "en": "Tokyo"
    },
    "subdivision": "tokyo",
    "highlights": {
      "zh": [
        "四季景观：春樱夏绿秋枫冬雪，每个季节皆有迥异的园林之美",
        "日式造园艺术：假山、石灯笼、锦鲤池等传统造园元素的精妙运用",
        "都市绿洲：繁忙城市中难得的清幽空间，可静享片刻宁静"
      ],
      "en": [
        "Seasonal scenery: cherry blossoms in spring, greenery in summer, foliage in autumn and snow in winter",
        "Japanese landscaping: artful use of rock arrangements, stone lanterns and koi ponds",
        "Urban oasis: a rare tranquil retreat amid the busy city, ideal for a moment of calm"
      ]
    },
    "admission": {
      "zh": "约150—600日元（各园不同）",
      "en": "Typically ¥150–600 depending on the garden"
    },
    "duration": {
      "zh": "1—2小时",
      "en": "1–2 hours"
    },
    "tips": {
      "zh": "春季赏樱（3月底—4月初）和秋季赏枫（11月中旬）是最佳游览季节。",
      "en": "Cherry blossom (late March–early April) and autumn foliage (mid-November) are the most rewarding seasons."
    }

    "id": "mori-garden",

      "en": "Mōri Garden"
    },
    "description": {

      "en": "Mōri Garden is a fine garden in Tokyo that elegantly incorporates traditional Japanese landscape design. The grounds transform with each season, offering a peaceful urban retreat."

      "zh": "东京都",
      "en": "Tokyo"
    },

    "highlights": {
      "zh": [
        "四季景观：春樱夏绿秋枫冬雪，每个季节皆有迥异的园林之美",
        "日式造园艺术：假山、石灯笼、锦鲤池等传统造园元素的精妙运用",
        "都市绿洲：繁忙城市中难得的清幽空间，可静享片刻宁静"
      ],

      ]
    },

      "en": "1–2 hours"
    },
    "tips": {

    }

        "日式造园艺术：假山、石灯笼、锦鲤池等传统造园元素的精妙运用",

    },

      "en": "Rikugien Gardens is a fine garden in Tokyo that elegantly incorporates traditional Japanese landscape design. The grounds transform with each season, offering a peaceful urban retreat."
    },

    "tags": {

      ],

      ]
    },
    "location": {
      "zh": "东京都",
      "en": "Tokyo"
    },
    "subdivision": "tokyo",
    "highlights": {
      "zh": [
        "四季景观：春樱夏绿秋枫冬雪，每个季节皆有迥异的园林之美",
        "日式造园艺术：假山、石灯笼、锦鲤池等传统造园元素的精妙运用",
        "都市绿洲：繁忙城市中难得的清幽空间，可静享片刻宁静"
      ],
      "en": [
        "Seasonal scenery: cherry blossoms in spring, greenery in summer, foliage in autumn and snow in winter",
        "Japanese landscaping: artful use of rock arrangements, stone lanterns and koi ponds",
        "Urban oasis: a rare tranquil retreat amid the busy city, ideal for a moment of calm"
      ]
    },
    "admission": {
      "zh": "约150—600日元（各园不同）",

      "en": "1–2 hours"
    },
    "tips": {

      "en": "Cherry blossom (late March–early April) and autumn foliage (mid-November) are the most rewarding seasons."
    }
  },

      "zh": "涩谷Sky展望台",
      "en": "Shibuya Sky"
    },
    "description": {
      "zh": "涩谷Sky展望台是东京都的著名展望设施，可将城市全景尽收眼底，无论白天还是夜晚都是欣赏都市风光的绝佳场所。",
      "en": "Shibuya Sky is a celebrated observation facility in Tokyo, offering sweeping panoramic views across the city both by day and after dark."
    },
    "image": "https://picsum.photos/seed/shibuya-sky-japan/800/500",
    "tags": {
      "zh": [
        "展望台",
        "全景",
        "地标"
      ],
      "en": [

        "Panoramic View",
        "Landmark"
      ]
    },
    "location": {
      "zh": "东京都",
      "en": "Tokyo"
    },
    "subdivision": "tokyo",
    "highlights": {
      "zh": [
        "全景视野：360度或广角俯瞰城市天际线，可一览城市全貌",
        "昼夜景观：晴天可能看到富士山，夜间璀璨夜景同样令人难忘",
        "建筑体验：展望台所在建筑本身亦是令人印象深刻的地标"
      ],
      "en": [
        "Panoramic views: 360° or wide-angle vista over the city skyline",
        "Day and night: clear days may offer a view of Mt Fuji; the evening city lights are spectacular",
        "The building itself: the structure housing the observation deck is often an architectural landmark"
      ]

    "admission": {
      "zh": "约1,000—2,500日元",
      "en": "Typically ¥1,000–2,500"

      "zh": "30分钟—1小时",
      "en": "30 minutes–1 hour"
    },
    "tips": {
      "zh": "日落时分（黄昏）是兼顾白天景色与夜景的最佳观赏时机。",
      "en": "Sunset (dusk) is ideal for combining daytime views with the onset of city lights."
    }

        "日式造园艺术：假山、石灯笼、锦鲤池等传统造园元素的精妙运用",

    "subdivision": "tokyo",
    "highlights": {
      "zh": [
        "四季景观：春樱夏绿秋枫冬雪，每个季节皆有迥异的园林之美",
        "日式造园艺术：假山、石灯笼、锦鲤池等传统造园元素的精妙运用",
        "都市绿洲：繁忙城市中难得的清幽空间，可静享片刻宁静"
      ],
      "en": [
        "Seasonal scenery: cherry blossoms in spring, greenery in summer, foliage in autumn and snow in winter",
        "Japanese landscaping: artful use of rock arrangements, stone lanterns and koi ponds",
        "Urban oasis: a rare tranquil retreat amid the busy city, ideal for a moment of calm"
      ]
    },
    "admission": {
      "zh": "约150—600日元（各园不同）",
      "en": "Typically ¥150–600 depending on the garden"
    },
    "duration": {
      "zh": "1—2小时",
      "en": "1–2 hours"
    },
    "tips": {
      "zh": "春季赏樱（3月底—4月初）和秋季赏枫（11月中旬）是最佳游览季节。",
      "en": "Cherry blossom (late March–early April) and autumn foliage (mid-November) are the most rewarding seasons."
    }
  },
